## Current judgment

**Long bonds still clear at expensive yields; the weak credit tail remains the warning.** Monday10/19 Treasury10Y rose6bp, entirely real-led on fitted-par subtraction; 30Y rose5bp, also real-led. ACM October7→16 attributes the fitted10Y fall to premium compression while expected-rate component rose; model accounting does not establish buyback or haven causation.

**Credit is diverging by quality.** ICE/FRED October19 HY329bp (−2 daily), CCC1310 (+13), IG87 (+2). HY remains21bp below350, +66 from263 and9–34bp short of BOND's credit-equity lead band. Wider credit alongside last week's lower real yields defeats a simple rates-only explanation. No broad issuance freeze established: SIFMA through September is lagged aggregate evidence; a current pulled-deal census is missing.

**Dealer evidence is selective.** Latest published as-of10/7 Treasury long-end stock$135.084B (+$2.022B), one build, not two. LIQUID's primary-backed below-IG corporate net stock fell$2.795B in that same lagged week. Net stocks cannot identify gross forced sales, shorts or capacity refusal. SOFR−IORB −3bp [10/19] gives no acute median repo stress; private/offshore channels remain unobserved.

**Treasury's October16 dealer agenda asks about buyback effects and structural demand, not a specific20Y suspension.** The cut story remains speculation; November4 resolves F1/F3. ## Current judgment

**WAVE-2026-10 is open and reads MECHANICAL so far (Log4Shell-shaped). Not one of the nine escalation rows has fired (rows 1-8 re-checked 2026-10-23; workbook brought current 15:5x ET: KB-CYP-022..031, CYB-INC-025, VX evidence, EXIT_PROTOCOL context note).** PHP2Shell (CVE-2026-109618 / -109619) is **ATTEMPTS-ONLY**. Exploitation attempts are confirmed: AWS MadPot from 10/17, before the 10/21 disclosure, and watchTowr honeypots since 10/21; Bitdefender and Huntress telemetry see none, and Sansec says public exploits fail on a default PHP install (vendor claims). No named organisation has a confirmed successful compromise. The Dutch NCSC is "investigating first signals of successful misuse" (primary, page unchanged since 10/22 16:01 CEST at the 10/23 07:3x ET re-read) and names no victim. **New reach (primary, 10/23):** Palo Alto says PAN-OS, Panorama and WildFire WF-500 are affected; hotfixes due 10/26–10/30, Panorama / WF-500 "ETA: TBD"; no exploitation known against its products. **That is weak evidence, not an all-clear.** Pre-disclosure exploitation plus a low-footprint exploit (KB-CYP-012) is the MOVEit shape, where victims surfaced at +14 days. The prior from five past waves is mechanical for banks (KB-CYP-001: 0 of 5 caused a run, a failure or a central payment outage).

## CONVERGENCE MATRIX (blueprint §2 handle — added 2026-10-14, s029)

> **One rule.** Each row names its **source** cell first under *Local state*; the score is that cell's colour: **5 🔴🔴 · 4 🔴 · 3 🟠 · 2 🟡 or LOW · 1 🟢 / ⚪ / DORMANT / DISCONFIRMED**. Other labels on the vector are listed, never scored. Transmission vectors take their source from THESIS § TRANSMISSION CHAIN. A score moves only when its source cell's colour moves (closeout step 1); the triggers are the registered events that would move the source. Conflicts between OTTO's own cells are recorded here and owed to THESIS v1.6, not settled by this table.
> Index over the local forms; the dashboard is unchanged. ACTIVE VECTORS folded in, labels carried (old table: cold §15). Stages [5] BDC 🔴 and [6] PC gating 🔴🔴 are BROCK's, not scored.
